+3.12.3 Disable password verification
+
+You can go to DEVICE >> Quick switch >> Password verification to disable password verification. Once password
+verification disabled, there will no need to input password when you connect with Beacon.
+Remark: If password verification feature was disabled, you will not be able to change password or reset Beacon due
+to security process restrictions.
+
+3.12.4 Disable scan response packet
+
+As we have described previously, advertisement format is consistent of ADV_IND and SCAN_RSP. In order to save the
+power consumption, we also provided the switch to turn on/off the scan response packet.
+You can access to ÔÇ£DEVICE >> Quick switch >> Scan response packetÔÇØ to disable it and then relevant information
+which included in scan response packet will not be shown in the ÔÇ£SCANNING PREVIEWÔÇØ interface.
+
+3.13 Remote turn off your Beacon
+
+In some cases that mechanical button function should be disabled to avoid intentional shut down situations, then we
+will need to remote shut down function for backup.
+Under this circumstance, you can remote turn off your Beacon by just tapping ÔÇ£DEVICE >>> Turn off BeaconÔÇØ option,
+then it will give prompt to you to confirm the shut down operations.
+
+           NOTE ÔÇô Please make sure that device have mechanical button, or other ways to turn on the Beacon
+           before you do remote shut down operations.
+
+3.14 Remote reset your Beacon
+
+In some cases that mechanical button function should be disabled or without mechanical button, to avoid intentional
+reset situations, then we will need remote reset function for backup. You can remote reset your Beacon by just
+tapping ÔÇ£DEVICE >>> Reset BeaconÔÇØ option, then it will give prompt to you to confirm the reset operations.
+Remark: Remote reset operations will not change the password to factory reset status. If you want to totally reset the
+Beacon with password restore as well, then you may need to do hardware reset operations.
+
+3.15 Modify ÔÇ£Connection passwordÔÇØ
+
+In MOKO firmware, password verification is ON by default and ÔÇ£Moko4321ÔÇØ was set as default connection password.
+You can modify your BeaconÔÇÖs connection password by just tapping ÔÇ£DEVICE >>> modify passwordÔÇØ option, and then
+input the password as you want.
+Remark: Connection password should not exceed 16 characters in length.
+
+                                                                                      31
+                                                                       Copyright ┬® 2025 MOKO TECHNOLOGY LTD.
+                                                                                                                MOKO Button APP User Manual V1.2
+
+3.16 Modify ÔÇ£Device NameÔÇØ
+
+As we have described in the content parsing of Beacon advertisement, ÔÇ£Device nameÔÇØ was set as a global variable
+and contained in the ÔÇ£Alarm infoÔÇØ advertising packet, so you can access to ÔÇ£DEVICE >>> Device NameÔÇØ to full-fill with
+1-10 ASCII characters.
+
+3.17 Modify ÔÇ£Device IDÔÇØ
+
+As we have described in the content parsing of Beacon advertisement, ÔÇ£Device IDÔÇØ was set as a global variable and
+contained in the ÔÇ£Alarm infoÔÇØ advertisement packet. It was designed to adapt some special rules of serial ID or
+combination of Major/Minor and etc., so you can access to ÔÇ£DEVICE >>> Device IDÔÇØ to full-fill with 1-6 bytes
+hexadecimal values.
+
+3.18 Check your device information
+
+In some cases, you will need to know the firmware version of Beacon, or product model and etc. You can access to
+ÔÇ£Device >>> Device infoÔÇØ interface to check and confirm these essential information.
+In customization firmware, these information can also be edit as customer required.

+         the upgrade to complete, APP will disconnect with Beacon after upgrade completed successfully.

+4. Common instructions of ÔÇ£nRF connectÔÇØ APP
+
+In case of some customers who using the ÔÇ£nRF connectÔÇØ APP to compatible with different vendorsÔÇÖ Beacon, here we

+4.1 How to unlock device (password verification)?
+
+Due to most operations based on device unlocked status, so here described the steps to unlock device based on
+Android nRF Connect APP in order to continue other operations further.
+For iOS nRF Connect APP, you can also follow this similar steps with same SERVICES and CHARACTERISTICS. This
+chapter will not give example of password verification for iOS nRF Connect APP again.
+Step 1: Connect device - Ôæá.
+Step 2: Click ÔÇ£configuration ServiceÔÇØ- UUID: 0000AA00-0000-1000-8000-00805f9b34fb and then turn on the ÔÇ£notifyÔÇØ
+
+         property in unlock characteristic - UUID: 0000AA07-0000-1000-8000-00805f9b34fb - Ôæí first.
+Step 3: Write unlock property - Ôæó and there will have a prompt. In this step , you need to convert password (ASCII)
+
+         into HEX first and then input in the option box. In this step , we have adopted customized password
+         verification protocol, and the details as below:
+     ´âÿ Password verification protocol : EA0155084D6F6B6F34333231
+
+           ´ü¼ ÔÇ£EA0155ÔÇØ ---- Fixed protocol header.
+           ´ü¼ ÔÇ£08ÔÇØ ---- Password length (need to be coordinate with password).
+           ´ü¼ ÔÇ£4D6F6B6F34333231ÔÇØ ---- Password ASCII ÔÇ£Moko4321ÔÇØ in HEX format.
+For instance´╝ÜIf you are using default connection password in the firmware ÔÇô ÔÇ£Moko4321ÔÇØ (ASCII), the corresponding
+HEX is ÔÇ£4d6f6b6f34333231ÔÇØ, then you will need to input ÔÇ£EA0155084D6F6B6F34333231ÔÇØ accordingly.
+Step 4: After unlock value input and then it will shows ÔÇ£EB-01-55-01-AAÔÇØ to indicate it is unlocked.
+NOTE: If you disabled password verification in the Beacon, it will be no need to do ÔÇ£unlock deviceÔÇØ operations.
+
+Figure 26: Workflow of unlocking device in android nRF Connect APP
+
+                                                                                      35
+                                                                       Copyright ┬® 2025 MOKO TECHNOLOGY LTD.
+                                                                                                                MOKO Button APP User Manual V1.2
+
+4.2 How to modify device name?
+
+Step 1: Connect device.
+Step 2: Unlock device. Please refer to chapter 4.1 ÔÇô How to unlock device.
+Step 3: Modify device name
+Ôæá Assume that you will need to modify device name into ÔÇ£Moko4321ÔÇØ, then convert into HEX ÔÇô ÔÇ£4d6f6b6f34333231ÔÇØ
+Ôæí Find ÔÇ£ADV Slot DataÔÇØ characters in ÔÇ£Eddystone configuration ServiceÔÇØ, then input ÔÇ£404d6f6b6f34333231ÔÇØ and
+press OK. Device name will be shown as ÔÇ£Moko4321ÔÇØ.

+Step 5: Click the DFU button on the top of the screen and select the Distribution packet (ZIP) from the file type list,

+Step 6: The upgrade process will start automatically and wait for the upgrade to complete, APP will disconnect with

+4.4 How to listening button event in Long connection mode
+
+Step 1: Connect device.
+Step 2: Unlock device. Please refer to chapter 4.1 ÔÇô How to unlock device.
+Step 3: Find ÔÇ£AA08ÔÇØ characters, then enable the notify property.
+
+         For exmaple: EB 02 06 01 03 (The last byte indicates the number of button click times)
+
+4.5 How to clear button click times in Long connection mode
+
+Step 1: Connect device.
+Step 2: Unlock device. Please refer to chapter 4.1 ÔÇô How to unlock device.
+Step 3: Find ÔÇ£AA08ÔÇØ characters, then enable the notify property.
+
+         RX: EB 02 06 01 03 (The last byte indicates the number of button click times)
+Step 4: Find ÔÇ£AA01ÔÇØ characters,
+
+         Ôæá Enable the notify property of AA01.
+         Ôæí Write the remote alarm cancellation protocol to the feature value AA01
+
+                  TX: EA 01 41 00
+                  RX: EB 01 41 01 AA (The last byte return: AA -- Success ; 00 -- Fail)
+Step 5: ÔÇ£AA08ÔÇØ characters return: EB 02 06 01 00
+
+4.6 How to configure broadcast battery voltage (Alarm info)
+
+Step 1: Connect device.
+Step 2: Unlock device. Please refer to chapter 4.1 ÔÇô How to unlock device.
+Step 3: Find ÔÇ£AA01ÔÇØ characters,
+
+         Ôæá Enable the notify property of AA01.
+         Ôæí Write the remote alarm cancellation protocol to the feature value AA01
+
+                  TX: EA 01 61 01 01 (00: battery percentage; 01 - battery voltage)
+                  RX: EB 01 61 01 AA (The last byte return: AA -- Success ; 00 -- Fail)
+
+                                                                                      38
+                                                                       Copyright ┬® 2025 MOKO TECHNOLOGY LTD.
+                                                                                                                MOKO Button APP User Manual V1.2
+
+4.7 How to parse button trigger event in Advertising mode?
+
+If you want to parse the button trigger events in the advertising mode, it is recommended to enable the channel's
+ÔÇ£Alarm modeÔÇØ and ÔÇ£Stay advertising before triggerÔÇØ function. This allows the device to broadcast both before and
+after a button event occurs. To distinguish whether the button event has occurred, you can determine it through the
+relevant bytes in the broadcast format. The methods for determining different broadcast types are as follows:
+
+     ´ü« Alarm info: Bit1 of the Byte8 in the Alarm info frame in the broadcast format.
+
+´ü« iBeacon: The broadcast parameters after the trigger will automatically increment the first byte of the
+     iBeacon UUID parameter you configured by 1.
+
+       iBeacon parameters  Before the button trigger After the button trigger
+
+                           event occurs              event occurs
+
+UUID   01BBCCDD-AABB-CCDD-AABB- 01BBCCDD-AABB-CCDD-  02BBCCDD-AABB-CCDD-
+                                                     AABB-CCDDAABBCCDD
+       CCDDAABBCCDD        AABB-CCDDAABBCCDD
+
+Major  1                   1                         1
+
+Minor  1                   1                         1
+
+´ü« Eddystone-UID: After the button trigger event, the last two bytes of the Eddystone-UID broadcast frame
+     will be 0x00 01. Before the button trigger event, the last two bytes of the Eddystone-UID broadcast frame is
+     0x00 00.
+
+                                39
+          Copyright ┬® 2025 MOKO TECHNOLOGY LTD.
+                                                                                                                MOKO Button APP User Manual V1.2
+
+5. FAQ
+
+General Questions
+´ü¼ What is RSSI?
+The RSSI (Received Signal Strength Indication) is a measurement of the power present in a received radio signal. It is
+usually displayed in dBm (Decibel-milliwatts). Low power devices, like Bluetooth Low Energy devices, are using very
+week power to transfer data, therefore the usual values of RSSI are between -100dBm (Very week signal) to around -
+20 dBm (strong signal).
+´ü¼ Why the RSSI values are different on two Android devices.
+By definition the RSSI value is a measurement of the power present in received radio signal. The signal strength
+depends on the antennas, device orientation, location, distance from the phone to target device, humidity etc.
+´ü¼ WhatÔÇÖs the difference between Advertising data and Scan Response data?
+In Bluetooth 4+ device, if not connected, continuously advertises with the Advertising data. When a scanner receives
+it, it may send the Scan request packet to obtain more advertising information. Then, the advertising device sends
+the Scan response data. Both packets may have up to 31 bytes.
+APP Questions
+´ü¼ Why the device name shows N/A?
+Device name is contained in scan response packet in ÔÇ£Device infoÔÇØ advertisement frame type, if ÔÇ£Device infoÔÇØ slot is
+not set, then APP will not achieve the device name and will shows N/A accordingly.
+

+2. Content parsing of Beacon advertisement
+
+Before introducing the APP user manual, we will guide you to fully understand the Beacon advertisement format and
+content parsing rules. In terms of the MOKO ÔÇ£BXP-TÔÇØ series firmware, which is especially design for asset tracking, it
+contains several kinds of advertisement frame type which include standard format and customized format, that is:
+ ´âÿ Google EddystoneTM - UID
+ ´âÿ Google EddystoneTM - URL
+ ´âÿ Google EddystoneTM - TLM (Unencrypted)
+ ´âÿ Customized advertisement frame ÔÇô ÔÇ£iBeaconÔÇØ
+ ´âÿ Customized advertisement frame - ÔÇ£Tag infoÔÇØ
+To improve the readability of the screen formats, this document presents:
+
+  - The fixed Bluetooth fields highlighted in blue.
+  - The variable Bluetooth fields highlighted in orange.
+  - User-defined/configurable fields highlighted in green.

+The screenshots have been taken on the ÔÇ£nRF ConnectÔÇØ application, developed by Nordic Semiconductors.

+3. ÔÇ£MOKO TAGÔÇØ APP user guidance
+
+Well, you have already familiar with the advertisement format and well to know how to parse the content in the
+advertisement, now we will guide you to know the ÔÇ£MOKO TAGÔÇØ APP and letÔÇÖs do some configuration on the Tag. For
+the latest APP, please search ÔÇ£MOKO TAGÔÇØ and download from Google play store or Apple store.
+This user guidance for ÔÇ£MOKO TAGÔÇØ app is based on Android/iOS APP version 1.0.0 or above, and the content of this
+user guidance are subject to change without prior notice for further improvement.
+
+ 3.1 Discover your tag
+
+The smartphone with ÔÇ£MOKO TAGÔÇØ APP (Android or iOS) installed can be as a Bluetooth Center to scan and discover
+the advertisement packets of Bluetooth Low Energy (BLE) Beacons, and it will provide multiple strategies to filter the
+Bluetooth packets and that can help you to capture the required BLE peripherals directly and quickly.
+
+ 3.1.1 Scanning your tag
+
+When enter into the ÔÇ£SCANNING PREVIEWÔÇØ interface, the SCANNER will keep scanning continuously and list all
+surrounding advertising Bluetooth LE peripherals which integrated with ÔÇ£BXP-TÔÇØ series firmware, unless stop
+scanning manually by pressing scanning button ÔÇ£ ÔÇØ.
+In this SCANNER interface, you will see device advertisement preview to recognize your Beacon device. To distinguish
+specific type of devices separately, only Beacon devices with MOKO ÔÇ£BXP-TÔÇØ series firmware can be shown in the APP
+preview interface and others will be filtered automatically.
+Below picture shows the example scanning preview of MOKO Tag series products.
+
+                                        Figure 3: Example scanning preview of MOKO TAG
+
+ 3.1.2 Filtering your tag
+
+To find your tag quickly, we have developed multiple strategies to filter among mass Beacon devices, users may
+narrow your device list by BLE name, MAC address and RSSI value through ÔÇ£Edit FilterÔÇØ option.
+
+                                                                                      16
+                                                                       Copyright ┬® 2022 MOKO TECHNOLOGY LTD.
+          MOKO TAG APP User Manual V1.0
+
+BLE Name  Allow to input 1-20 characters.
+Mac Addr
+Min.RSSI  Example: Only Beacon devices whose BLE name contain ÔÇ£MOKOÔÇØ will
+          be displayed in SCANNER interface.
+
+          NOTE´╝ÜSpace effective, and case insensitive as well.
+
+          Allow to input 1-6 bytes (HEX).
+
+          Example: Only Beacon devices whose Mac address contain ÔÇ£B3:E8ÔÇØ will
+          be displayed in SCANNER interface.
+
+          NOTE´╝ÜThere is no need to input colon between MAC address in search
+          filter column, and case insensitive as well.
+
+          Only the advertisement packets with RSSI not less than the Min.RSSI
+          value will be displayed in SCANNER interface, and configuration range
+          is -100dBm to 0dBm.
+
+          Example: When setting the Min.RSSI value to -70, the ADV packets
+          with RSSI of -70dBm or greater than -70dBm will be list in SCANNER
+          interface.
+
+Figure 4: Configurations of scanning filter settings
+
+NOTE:
+1 RSSI is the highest priority filtering condition. It means that filtered device must meet the RSSI filtering condition.
+2 There is an ÔÇ£ORÔÇØ relationship between BLE Name and Mac Addr. It means that filtered device just need meet one of
+
+    the filtering conditions.
+
+ 3.2 Connect with your tag
+
+For the Tag with ÔÇ£BXP-TÔÇØ series firmware, it will display ÔÇ£CONNECTÔÇØ button in the ÔÇ£SCANNING PREVIEWÔÇØ interface if
+the status is connectable and you can follow below steps to connect with your tag:
+Step 1: Press ÔÇ£CONNECTÔÇØ button.
+Step 2: Input connection password and then press ÔÇ£OKÔÇØ button, Green LED will blink 2 times rapidly again.
+
+           Notice ÔÇô Default password in MOKO standard firmware is ÔÇ£Moko4321ÔÇØ.
+
+ 3.3 Know your tagÔÇÖs advertising logic
+
+Before setting up your tagÔÇÖs advertisement, you may need to understand the basic advertising logic and working
+flowchart of the tag. With the integration of ultra-long advertising interval (standby duration) feature, in order to
+guarantee the efficiency of connections based on ultra-long advertising interval mode, we have integrated a constant
+advertising cycle which is called configuration period when device boot up.
+
+                                                                                      17
+                                                                       Copyright ┬® 2022 MOKO TECHNOLOGY LTD.
+                                                                          MOKO TAG APP User Manual V1.0
+
+The configuration period will last for 30 seconds and then device will start working (advertising period) by following
+the rules you preset in the advertisement SLOTs.
+
+Remark: If standby duration is set into 0, then adv duration value will be invalid and device will keep advertising no
+matter what adv duration value you set.
+
+                                     Overall advertising logic
+
+ Advertising Period   Device status  Default Configurations
+Configuration period
+ Advertising period                  Adv interval ÔÇô 1000ms (non-configurable)
+
+                      Connectable                           Adv duration ÔÇô 30s (non-configurable)
+                                     *If you have modification requirements on this, please contact with sales for
+
+                                                                support.
+
+                                     Adv interval ÔÇô 1000ms (configurable)
+
+                      Connectable    Adv duration ÔÇô 10s (configurable)
+
+                                     Standby duration ÔÇô 0s (configurable)
+
+                      Table 6:Different adverting periods in overall advertising logic
+
+                                                  Figure 5: Overall advertising logic
+
+ 3.4 How to apply to your scenarios?
+
+To expand the usage scenarios of ÔÇ£MOKO TAGÔÇØ series products in asset management, we have implemented some
+specific features to make it preferentially and exclusively adapted to various asset tracking situations, such as
+movement-based tracking mode, asset presence detection and ultra-long advertising interval etc.
+Here we will introduce some typical scenarios for your reference based on practical cooperations with end users. In
+order to ensure the feasibility of settings for all users in different application scenarios, we do not directly define
+the mode switch but need users to configure relevant parameters to achieve this. Under this circumstance, we have
+also made corresponding flowchart to elaborate the details and guide you how to set this in the APP interface.
+´âÿPresence detection mode
+
+                                                                                      18
+                                                                       Copyright ┬® 2022 MOKO TECHNOLOGY LTD.
+                                       MOKO TAG APP User Manual V1.0
+
+´âÿMovement-based dynamic tracking mode
+
+3.4.1 Presence detection mode
+
+This presence detection mode is commonly used for fixed assets, such as furniture/desks/photocopiers in the office,
+or medical equipments/beds in the hospital. In these practical scenarios, you will only need to know it is in right
+place rather than the real-time positions, so many end users prefer to keep device broadcasting every 1 hour or more.
+
+Below described the typical configurations and working logical of presence detection mode:
+
+          items                             Typical scenario 1 ÔÇô Presence detection               Configurations
+   Working logical                                          Descriptions                                  -
+
+Typical configurations  Device will broadcast 10s with 1000ms advertising interval every 1 hour.      1000ms
+                                                        Advertising interval                             10s
+    Motion sensor                                      Advertising duration
+ Suggested scenarios                                     Standby duration                              3600s
+                                                           Not necessary                                  -
+
+                                   Smart office ÔÇô Furniture/desks/photocopiers tracking                   -
+                                       Healthcare ÔÇô Medical equipment/beds tracking
+
+                        Table 7:Typical parameters configuration of presence detect mode
+
+                              Figure 6: Advertising workflow of typical presence detection mode
+
+ 3.4.2 Movement-based dynamic tracking mode
+
+This dynamic tracking mode is commonly used for RTLS application scenarios, such as packaging box/container/pallet
+in the warehouse, frequently used IV infusion pumps/wheelchairs in the hospital, or shopping carts in the market. In
+these practical scenarios, you must need to know its real-time positions rather than simple presence status, and in
+order to save the battery consumption when assets in no use status (detected by motion sensor), you can lower the
+
+                                                                                      19
+                                                                       Copyright ┬® 2022 MOKO TECHNOLOGY LTD.
+                                                                                MOKO TAG APP User Manual V1.0
+
+advertisement report frequency when assets in long-terms idle status. Based on these demands, many clients prefer
+the movement-based dynamic tracking mode.
+
+Below described the typical configurations and working logical of dynamic tracking mode:
+
+                        Typical scenario 2 ÔÇô Movement-based dynamic tracking mode

+ 3.5 Access to sensor configurations
+
+ 3.5.1 Motion sensor (3-axis accelerometer)
+
+Regarding of MOKO TAG series product, currently we have adopted motion sensor for ÔÇ£Movement-based dynamic
+trackingÔÇØ functions which link to ÔÇ£Chapter 3.4.2 : Movement-based dynamic tracking modeÔÇØ for your reference, and
+you can access to ÔÇ£SETTING >>>Sensor configurations>>> 3-axis accelerometerÔÇØ interface for further configurations.
+Remark: Configuration interface will be shown only when device equipped with 3-axis accelerometer sensor.
+
+                                          Figure 8: Configurations page of motion sensor
+
+                                                                                      21
+                                                                       Copyright ┬® 2022 MOKO TECHNOLOGY LTD.
+                                                                                                                MOKO TAG APP User Manual V1.0
+
+3.5.1.1 Static heartbeat
+
+This feature is mainly applicable in situations where the device will remain stationary for a very long time. After
+reaching the fixed static duration condition, the device will start a fixed duration broadcast to ensure that terminal
+users can get the online status of the device, it just like the deviceÔÇÖs ÔÇ£heartbeat broadcastÔÇØ. This feature is a global
+configuration for the mobile trigger function and it is disabled by default.
+If you want to enable this feature, you need to understand the prerequisites for enabling it. This is very important,
+otherwise you will not be able to properly use it.
+1. The "Static heartbeat" feature requires that all active Slots have enabled the Motion detection function.
+2. The configuration of the Static heartbeat function's Static cycle time(T3) has a constraint condition: First, you need
+comparing and calculating the maximum value of the ÔÇ£keep staticÔÇØ time (T1) configured for the Motion detection
+function parameters in all activated Slots, the parameter T3 needs to be greater than this maximum value.
+3. Once the Static heartbeat function is enabled, the "Static heartbeat" advertising type will be broadcast according
+to the first enabled slot's broadcast type and broadcast interval, and the broadcast duration is the duration of the
+Adv duration time configured on the page for the Static heartbeat function.
+
+                               Figure 9: Configurations example instruction of Static heartbeat
+You can refer to the above picture to understand the configuration method of this feature. For example, if the
+configuration option only enables two Slots, Slot1's configured ÔÇ£keep staticÔÇØ time is 10 seconds, and Slot2's ÔÇ£keep
+staticÔÇØ time is 20 seconds, the ÔÇ£Static cycle timeÔÇØ of the Static heartbeat function needs to be greater than 20
+seconds, for instance, you can set ÔÇ£Static cycle timeÔÇØ to 1 hour.
+The working logic after configuring the above picture is as follows: if the device remains static for a long time and not
+move, the device will broadcast for 10 second every 1 hour at the interval of 1000ms, and the advertising type is Tag
+info.
+
+                                                                                      22
+                                                                       Copyright ┬® 2022 MOKO TECHNOLOGY LTD.
+                                                                                                                MOKO TAG APP User Manual V1.0
+
+ 3.5.2 Magnetic sensor (hall sensor)
+
+Currently we have adopted the same IO in the hardware design for magnetic sensor and mechanical button (if device
+have), so we have designed a function switch for multiple use as a matter of course.
+Feature 1: Magnetic sensor for power on/off only.
+Feature 2: Magnetic sensor for power on and trigger event detection only.
+For more details or acquiring the real-time magnetic sensor working status, you can access to ÔÇ£SETTING >>>Sensor
+configurations>>> Hall sensorÔÇØ.
+Remark: This feature is only applicable for the device which equipped with magnetic sensor.
+
+                                        Figure 10:Configurations page of magnetic sensor
+
+ 3.6 How to enable ÔÇ£Long rangeÔÇØ features?
+
+In order to enhance the transmission range of the tag and expand the application scenarios as well as reduce the
+gateway deployment costs, we have optimized the firmware feature and integrated with coded PHY mode. Based on
+current firmware, ÔÇ£1M PHY ÔÇô LegacyÔÇØ and ÔÇ£Coded PHY ÔÇô Long RangeÔÇØ are supported already, and you can access to
+ÔÇ£SETTING >>>ADV modeÔÇØ and adopt the right one to your application scenarios.
+
+                                                                                      23
+                                                                       Copyright ┬® 2022 MOKO TECHNOLOGY LTD.
+                                                                              MOKO TAG APP User Manual V1.0
+
+                          Figure 11:How to enable ÔÇ£Long RangeÔÇØ mode?
+
+´âÿLegacy --- 1M PHY
+´âÿLong Rang --- Coded PHY
+
+Normally the transmission range in Coded PHY mode will be multiple times of transmission range in 1M PHY, and the
+advertisement payload format/content will be remaining with only the changes of advertisement type due to BLE 5.0
+new specifications.
+
+Here we have listed the ADV type difference between 1M PHY and Coded PHY. And for more introduction you can
+refer to link: https://www.bluetooth.com/bluetooth-resources/intro-to-bluetooth-low-energy-coded-phy/
+
+                                                                   PHYs
+
+         Frame type                           LE 1M                             LE Coded
+
+           iBeacon        Connectable status  Non-connectable status Connectable status Non-connectable status
+Eddystone ÔÇô URL/TLM/UID
+                          ADV_IND + SCAN_RSP         ADV_SCAN_IND             ADV_EXT_IND + AUX_ADV_IND
+           Tag info
+                          ADV_IND             ADV_NONCONN_IND                 ADV_EXT_IND + AUX_ADV_IND
+
+                          ADV_IND + SCAN_RSP         ADV_SCAN_IND             ADV_EXT_IND + AUX_ADV_IND
+
+                          Table 9:ADV type difference between 1M and Coded PHY
+

+disable/enable these features through the switch, thus adapting to different use cases.

+You can enable/disable these function switch in ÔÇ£Setting >>> Quick switchÔÇØ section, and below shows the ÔÇ£Quick
+switchÔÇØ interface as well as relevant feature descriptions.

+                          Figure 12: Configurations page of Quick switch

+ 3.7.1 Switch your Tag to unconnectable status
+
+In some cases, you will need to disable the tagÔÇÖs connectable property, to let it work just as a location tag. So you can
+disable the connectable status in ÔÇ£Setting >>> Quick switch >>> Connectable statusÔÇØ. After feature disabled, device
+wonÔÇÖt be connected by master BLE device anymore unless reset your tag.

+You can go to ÔÇ£Setting >> Quick switch >> Password verificationÔÇØ to disable password verification. Once password
+verification be disabled, there will be no need to input password when you connect with your tag.
+Remark: If password verification feature was disabled, you will not be able to change password or reset Beacon due
+to security process restrictions.

+Considering the invisibility in some specific scenarios or the save of battery consumption, you can access to ÔÇ£Setting
+>> Quick switch >> Trigger LED indicatorÔÇØ to disable the LED notification in trigger mode.

+                                         Figure 15: How to disable trigger LED indicator?

+ 3.8 Factory reset your Beacon
+
+ 3.8.1 Remote reset
+
+In some cases that mechanical button function should be disabled, to avoid intentional reset situations. Under this
+circumstance we will need remote reset function for backup. You can remote reset your tag by just connect with your
+tag and tapping ÔÇ£Setting >>> Reset BeaconÔÇØ option, then it will give prompt to you to confirm the reset operations.
+Remark: Remote reset operations will not change the connection password to factory reset status. If you want to
+totally reset the Beacon with password restore as well, then you may need to do hardware reset operations.
+
+ 3.8.2 Hardware reset
+
+In order to avoid the stuck in the dilemma of not being able to restore factory settings due to the hardware design
+without physical buttons, we have provided a universal operation flow to execute the hardware reset.
+Hardware reset operation flow:
+1)Remove the cover and the battery.
+2)Put the battery in until you see the LED keep blinking for 2 seconds. This LED blinking means that the battery is
+
+     connected successfully.
+3)When the LED blinking finishes, put the battery in again within 5 seconds, and then repeat the process four more
+
+     times, removing and replacing the battery. You should see a LED blinking each time you put the battery in, for a
+     total of five blinks. The fifth blinking is different from the previous four and indicates that the device is factory
+     reset successfully.
+
+           1st ÔÇô 4th : LED keep blinking for 2s. This indicates the battery is connected successfully.
+           5th : LED keep solid for 2s. This indicates the device is factory reset successfully.
+4)Replace the cover by aligning the battery room and press down on the cover.
+
+                             Figure 16:How to hardware reset you Tag without physical buttons?
+
+                                                                                      26
+                                                                       Copyright ┬® 2022 MOKO TECHNOLOGY LTD.
+                                                                                                                MOKO TAG APP User Manual V1.0
+
+ 3.9 Modify ÔÇ£Connection passwordÔÇØ
+
+In MOKO firmware, password verification is ON by default and ÔÇ£Moko4321ÔÇØ was set as default connection password.
+You can modify your BeaconÔÇÖs connection password by just tapping ÔÇ£DEVICE >>> modify passwordÔÇØ option, and then
+input the password as you want.
+Remark: Connection password should not exceed 16 characters in length.
+
+ 3.10 Remote control Beacon LED
+
+In some cases when you need to find your Beacon, then you can use this ÔÇ£Remote reminderÔÇØ functions. You can go
+to ÔÇ£SETTING >>> Remote reminderÔÇØ and then set the remote remind notification type, then just tap ÔÇ£RemindÔÇØ
+button, Beacon LED will start blinking according to the preset parameters.
+
+                                                   Figure 17:Remote reminder page
+In ÔÇ£Remote reminderÔÇØ page, there have two parameters that need to be set.
+
+     ´âÿBlinking time. ----- LED blinking duration after click the ÔÇ£RemindÔÇØ button in the app. Configuration range: 1 ÔÇô
+           6000 (Unit: 100ms)
+
+     ´âÿBlinking interval. ----- LED blinking interval. Configuration range: 1 ÔÇô 100 (Unit: 100ms)
+After configuration, you can control the LED of the beacon to blink by click theÔÇ£RemindÔÇØ button
+Remark: This operation is a one-time operation. Each time you click the Remind button, the LED will only be executed
+once based on the current configuration. In addition, please make sure your device is equipped with LED before
+performing this operation
+
+                                                                                      27
+                                                                       Copyright ┬® 2022 MOKO TECHNOLOGY LTD.
+                                                                                                                MOKO TAG APP User Manual V1.0
+
+ 3.11 Reset battery
+
+      As some of our products use lithium-ion batteries, the calculation method of this type of battery is different
+from that of button batteries. Therefore, for products using lithium-ion batteries, this option will be displayed in the
+APP. After changing the battery, you need to click this option to make the device to recalculate the battery power.
+
+                                                  Figure 18:Reset battery instruction
+Remark: If your product is the lithium sub-battery version, after replacing the battery, you need to connect the device
+and use this function to reset the battery of the device, otherwise, the battery power will not display the correct value.
+In addition, the button battery version of the product will not show this option box in the APP

+ 3.13 Upgrade your TagÔÇÖs firmware
+
+The tag can realize OTA firmware update through DFU operations. Regarding of different import methods for android
+app and iOS app, here we will describe the detailed steps separately.
+
+ 3.13.1 Android ÔÇô MOKO APP DFU instructions
+
+Step 1: Copy the upgrade package to a folder in the root directory of the smartphone.
+Step 2: Connect with device and go to ÔÇ£SETTINGÔÇØ interface, press ÔÇ£DFUÔÇØ option and then file manager route of
+
+         smartphone will be prompted.
+Step 3: Choose the required upgrade firmware package and the upgrade process will start automatically. Wait for the
+
+         upgrade to complete, APP will disconnect with Beacon after upgrade completed successfully.
+
+                        Figure 20: Firmware upgrade workflow of MOKO TAG application (Android)
+
+ 3.13.2 iOS ÔÇô MOKO APP DFU instructions
+
+Step 1: Install iTunes in PC and then import firmware upgrade package into ÔÇ£MOKO TAGÔÇØ APP, you can refer to below
+         picture demonstrations ÔÇô Figure 35: Load upgrade package to nRF connect APP via iTunes.
+
+Step 2: Connect with device and go to ÔÇ£SETTINGÔÇØ interface, press ÔÇ£DFUÔÇØ option and then firmware upgrade package
+         will be shown automatically in DFU path.
+
+Step 3: Choose the required upgrade firmware package and the upgrade process will start automatically. Wait for the
+         upgrade to complete, APP will disconnect with Beacon after upgrade completed successfully.
+
+                                                                                      29
+                                                                       Copyright ┬® 2022 MOKO TECHNOLOGY LTD.
+                                                                                                                MOKO TAG APP User Manual V1.0
+
+                               Figure 21: Load upgrade package to nRF connect APP via iTunes

+4. FAQ
+
+General Questions
+´ü¼What is RSSI?
+The RSSI (Received Signal Strength Indication) is a measurement of the power present in a received radio signal. It is
+usually displayed in dBm (Decibel-milliwatts). Low power devices, like Bluetooth Low Energy devices, are using very
+week power to transfer data, therefore the usual values of RSSI are between -100dBm (Very week signal) to around -
+20 dBm (strong signal).
+´ü¼Why the RSSI values are different on two Android devices.
+By definition the RSSI value is a measurement of the power present in received radio signal. The signal strength
+depends on the antennas, device orientation, location, distance from the phone to target device, humidity etc.
+´ü¼WhatÔÇÖs the difference between Advertising data and Scan Response data?
+In Bluetooth 4+ device, if not connected, continuously advertises with the Advertising data. When a scanner receives
+it, it may send the Scan request packet to obtain more advertising information. Then, the advertising device sends
+the Scan response data. Both packets may have up to 31 bytes.
+APP Questions
+´ü¼Why the device name shows N/A?
+Device name is contained in scan response packet in ÔÇ£Tag infoÔÇØ advertisement frame type, if ÔÇ£Tag infoÔÇØ slot is not set,
+then APP will not achieve the device name and will shows N/A accordingly.
+
+                                                                                      31
+                                                                       Copyright ┬® 2022 MOKO TECHNOLOGY LTD.
+                                                                             MOKO TAG APP User Manual V1.0
+

+1. Overview
+
+The MOKO PIR Sensor, which called ÔÇ£PIR PRESENCEÔÇØ is a battery-powered BLE4.2 product with passive infrared (PIR)
+sensor that provides real-time occupancy, presence detection as well as space utilization data for Enterprise IoT
+applications. The ÔÇ£PIR PRESENCEÔÇØ is designed for workplace and conference room monitoring and captures even the
+small movements of people working at desks.
+Integrated with passive infrared sensor, PIR PRESENCE can be utilized in smart lighting control, facility management
+and occupancy detection in various places such as offices, hospitals ,warehouse or intrusion alarm at home. Thanks
+to the outstanding low consumption of Bluetooth LE and large capacity of battery, PIR PRESENCE can be working
+continuously up to 5 years without replace battery.
+
+Figure 1: Typical application scenario of PIR PRESENCE
+
+                                                                                       6
+
+                                                                        Copyright ┬® 2023 MOKO TECHNOLOGY LTD.
+                                                                                                                     PIR PRESENCE User Manual V1.2
+

+3. How the PIR PRESENCE works?
+
+The PIR PRESENCE is based on passive infrared (PIR) as well as hall sensor, to detects changes in heat sources within
+its range (i.e., humans in motion) and hall sensor status respect to magnet (i.e., door closed/open). The typical logical
+is to detects the human movements as well as the hall sensor status, and then upload the behavior to the gateway
+node through BLE advertisement. So we will describe PIR motion detection, door open/close status detection and
+BLE advertisement separately.
+
+3.1 PIR Motion detection
+
+When the PIR sensor detected human movements, then it will output high level and device will report ÔÇ£1ÔÇØ in the BLE
+advertisement to the gateway for processing and delivery to cloud services.
+Below we have described the workflow of motion detection in PIR PRESENCE.
+
+Figure 4: Workflow of motion detection in PIR PRESENCE
+In above workflow, there have two important parameters related to working principle of PIR sensor that need to be
+paid more attention.
+Ô×ó Delay time* ÔÇô The time duration during which the output remains high after detecting human movements. In
+
+     other words, it can be configured and determines how long the PIR will keep high level after not detecting human
+     movements. The minimum delay time that we can set currently is 2 seconds (delay ÔÇô low level), and we have
+     provided three levels of delay time for you to pre-set and adapt to different scenarios.
+     You can refer to ÔÇ£chapter 6.4 ÔÇô Access to PIR and Hall sensorÔÇØ >> ÔÇ£DelayÔÇØ to configure this value.
+Ô×ó Lock time ÔÇô The time duration during which the PIR trigger is inhibited. In other words, the PIR will keep low level
+     during this period even though human movements are detected. This duration is fixed 2 seconds and cannot be
+     changed in current hardware design.
+
+*Remark: Delay time is highly related to hardware design. In current hardware design, delay time is also fixed and
+only have three levels available for you to pre-set. So if you want to get more delay response level or modify it, you
+can contact with us to do hardware modifications.
+
+                                                                                      10
+
+                                                                        Copyright ┬® 2023 MOKO TECHNOLOGY LTD.
+                                                                                                                     PIR PRESENCE User Manual V1.2
+
+3.2 Door open/close status detection
+
+In PIR PRESENCE, we have also integrated with hall sensor (need to be applied together with magnet accessory) to
+monitor the doorÔÇÖs open/close status.
+You can refer to ÔÇ£chapter 4.1 ÔÇô How to install PIR PRESENCEÔÇØ to install the PIR PRESENCE as well as magnet accessory.
+When magnet is approaching to hall sensor and distance is less than 20mm, hall sensor will output high level and
+device will report ÔÇ£1ÔÇØ in the BLE advertisement to the gateway for processing and delivery to cloud services.
+
+Figure 5: How to check doorÔÇÖs status in the advertisement?
+
+3.3 Bluetooth advertisement
+
+In last chapter, we have mentioned that the PIR PRESENCE is uploading the sensor status or other basic information
+through BLE advertisement, and those basic information can also be configured through MOKO ÔÇ£MK PIRÔÇØ APP. Before
+introducing the APP user manual, we will guide you to full understand the PIR advertisement format and content
+parsing rules.
+Regarding of PIR series firmware, there have only one customized advertisement format, which is a combination of
+ADV_IND (Advertising packet) and SCAN_RSP (Scan response packet). In the next we will describe the detailed
+information of these two packets.
+
+                                                                                      11
+
+                                                                        Copyright ┬® 2023 MOKO TECHNOLOGY LTD.
+                                                                                                                     PIR PRESENCE User Manual V1.2
+     Ô×ó ADV_IND (advertising packet) ---- Mainly to express status of PIR and hall sensor, battery voltage, Major,
+
+           Minor and etc.
+     Ô×ó SCAN_RSP (scan response packet) ---- Mainly to express Tx power and device name.
+Below picture showed the example content of Beacon advertisement:
+
+Figure 6: Example content of Beacon advertisement
+
+                                Table 7: Content structure of Customized-PIR advertising packet

+                             Table 8: Content structure of Customized-Device info response packet

+5.4 How to restore factory settings ?
+
+There have two ways to restore factory settings.
+Ô×ó Reset button (Hardware reset): Insert needle-like object into the reset hole to reset.
+Ô×ó ÔÇ£MK PIRÔÇØ APP Or Power Button (Software reset):
+
+     Option 1 - Connect PIR PRESENCE with ÔÇ£MK PIRÔÇØ APP and then execute ÔÇ£Remote resetÔÇØ operations to finish the
+     software reset.
+     Option 2 ÔÇô In power off status, long press button for more than 10s (during this period, LED will blink to indicate
+     the device ON, please ignore and keep long press) and then release when you see the LED solid lightening, to
+     finish the software reset.

+6. MOKO PIR APP user guidance
+
+This user guidance for ÔÇ£MK PIRÔÇØ APP is based on version 1.0.3 or above (only android application available now), and
+the contents of this user guidance are subject to change without prior notice for further improvement. For latest APP,
+please scan below QR code or search ÔÇ£MK PIRÔÇØ and download from Google play store.
+
+Figure 11: QR Code for downloading Android ÔÇ£MK PIRÔÇØ APP
+
+6.1 Discover your PIR device
+
+The smartphone with ÔÇ£MK PIRÔÇØ APP installed can be as a Bluetooth Center to scan and discover the advertisement
+packets of Bluetooth Low Energy (BLE) Beacons, and it will provide multiple strategies to filter the Bluetooth packets
+and that can help you to capture the required BLE peripherals directly and quickly.
+
+6.1.1 Scanning your PIR device
+
+When entering into the ÔÇ£SCANNING PREVIEWÔÇØ interface, the SCANNER will start scanning and list all surrounding
+advertising Bluetooth LE peripherals which integrated with MOKO PIR series firmware, and you can stop scanning
+manually by pressing scanning button ÔÇ£ ÔÇ£.
+In this SCANNER interface, you will see device advertisement preview to recognize your Beacon device. To distinguish
+specific type of Beacons separately, only PIR device can be shown in the APP preview interface and others will be
+filtered automatically. Below picture shows the default scanning preview of MOKO PIR PRESENCE device.
+
+Figure 12: Default scanning preview of PIR PRESENCE
+
+6.1.2 Filtering your PIR device
+
+To find your PIR device quickly, we have provided multiple strategies to filter among mass Beacon devices, users may
+narrow your device list by BLE name, MAC address and RSSI value through ÔÇ£Edit FilterÔÇØ option.
+
+                                                                                      16
+
+                                                                        Copyright ┬® 2023 MOKO TECHNOLOGY LTD.
+                                                                    PIR PRESENCE User Manual V1.2
+
+BLE Name  Allow to input 1-10 characters.
+Mac Addr
+Min.RSSI  Example: Only PIR devices whose BLE name contain ÔÇ£MOKOÔÇØ will be
+          displayed in SCANNER interface.
+
+          NOTE´╝ÜSpace effective, and case-insensitive as well.
+
+          Allow to input 1-6 bytes (HEX).
+
+          Example: Only PIR devices whose Mac address contain ÔÇ£B3:E8ÔÇØ will be
+          displayed in SCANNER interface.
+
+          NOTE´╝ÜThere is no need to input colon between MAC address in search
+          filter column, and case-insensitive as well.
+
+          Only the advertisement packets with RSSI not less than the Min.RSSI
+          value will be displayed in SCANNER interface , and configuration range
+          is -100dBm to 0dBm.
+
+          Example: When setting the Min.RSSI value to -70, the ADV packets
+          with RSSI of -70dBm or greater than -70dBm will be list in SCANNER
+          interface.
+
+Figure 13: Configurations of scanning filter settings
+
+NOTE:
+Ôæá RSSI is the highest priority filtering condition. It means that filtered device must meet the RSSI filtering condition.
+Ôæí There is an ÔÇ£ORÔÇØ relationship between BLE Name and Mac Addr. It means that filtered device just need meet one
+
+    of the filtering conditions.
+
+6.2 Connect with your PIR device
+
+For PIR devices, if the status is connectable, then it will shows ÔÇ£CONNECTÔÇØ button in the ÔÇ£SCANNING PREVIEWÔÇØ
+interface. You can follow below steps to connect with your Beacon device:
+Step 1: Press ÔÇ£CONNECTÔÇØ button, red LED will blink 2seconds rapidly.
+Step 2: Input connection password and then press ÔÇ£OKÔÇØ button, red LED will blink 2seconds rapidly again.
+
+Notice ÔÇô Default password in MOKO standard firmware is ÔÇ£Moko4321ÔÇØ.
+
+6.3 Getting your PIR device to broadcast
+
+Once device connected and the main screen of the ÔÇ£MK PIRÔÇØ APP appears, including ADVERTISEMENT interface,
+SETTING interface and DEVICE interface.
+Remark: If there are no write/read operations within 2 minutes after device connected, then it will be disconnected
+automatically to save power consumption.
+
+                                                                                      17
+
+                                                                        Copyright ┬® 2023 MOKO TECHNOLOGY LTD.
+                                                                                                                     PIR PRESENCE User Manual V1.2
+
+Figure 14: Configuration interface overview of main screen in ÔÇ£MK PIRÔÇØ APP
+
+6.3.1 Setting up advertisement content
+
+After you connected with device, then you can go to ÔÇ£ADVERTISEMENT >>> Adv contentÔÇØ part to configure the
+corresponding advertisement content. Details are as below.
+
+Figure 15: How to configure advertising content?
+     Ô×ó Major ---- You can defined the type value as you want in practical scenario, such as dividing region, group
+           or etc. Configuration range: 0-65535
+     Ô×ó Minor ---- You can defined the type value as you want in practical scenario, such as specific room, device or
+           etc. Configuration range: 0-65535
+     Ô×ó Device name ---- ASCII characters within 10 bytes.
+     Ô×ó Serial ID ---- Serial ID from 0 to 99999.
+
+Remark: Complete local name is a combination of ÔÇ£Device nameÔÇØ and ÔÇ£Serial IDÔÇØ. For instance, if you have set ÔÇ£PIRÔÇØ
+as device name, and ÔÇ£00001ÔÇØ as serial ID, then it will shows ÔÇ£PIR00001ÔÇØ in the preview interface. Or if you checked
+with ÔÇ£nRF ConnectÔÇØ APP, the complete local name will shows ÔÇ£PIR00001ÔÇØ.
+

+
+                                                                        Copyright ┬® 2023 MOKO TECHNOLOGY LTD.
+                                                                                                                     PIR PRESENCE User Manual V1.2

+6.3.2 Setting up advertising parameters
+
+After you connected with device, you can also go to ÔÇ£ADVERTISEMENT >>> Adv parametersÔÇØ part to configure the
+corresponding advertisement parameters. Details are as below.
+
+Figure 16: How to configure advertising parameters?
+     Ô×ó Adv Interval ---- The time between advertising packets. Configuration range: 100ms ÔÇô 10000ms.
+     Ô×ó RSSI@1m ---- Value that declares to receiving devices what the power should be at 1m distance from the
+           Beacon. Configuration range: -100dBm ÔÇô 0dBm.
+     Ô×ó Tx Power ---- Output power in number of dBm. Configuration level: -40/-20/-16/-12/-8/-4/0/+4 dBm.
+
+6.4 Access to PIR and Hall sensor
+
+Regarding of PIR PRESENCE product, currently we have adopted Passive infrared (PIR) sensor and Hall sensor for
+ÔÇ£presence detectionÔÇØ and ÔÇ£door/window intrusion alarmÔÇØ functions.
+You can go to ÔÇ£SETTING >>> PIR & HallÔÇØ interface for further configurations. For the detailed parameters, please refer

+6.5 Modify connection password
+
+In PIR PRESENCE firmware, ÔÇ£Moko4321ÔÇØ was set as default connection password. You can try to modify your BeaconÔÇÖs
+connection password by just tapping ÔÇ£SETTING >>> modify passwordÔÇØ option, and then input the password as you
+want.
+Remark: Connection password must be 8 characters in length.
+
+6.6 Switch your PIR device to unconnectable
+
+In some cases, you will need to disable the Beacon connectable property, to avoid high power consumption caused
+by deliberate connections. So you can disable the connectable status in ÔÇ£SETTING >>> ConnectableÔÇØ option. After
+disabled, device will not be connected by master BLE device any more unless reset the Beacon.
+Figure 18: Switch your PIR device to unconnectable status
+

+

+6.7 Disable button power off function
+
+Normally the button is used for power on/off purpose, however in some specific cases, there will need to disable the
+button function in order to avoid deliberate operations, so logically there also should have the corresponding switch
+to enable/disable it. You can go to ÔÇ£SETTING >>> Button Power OFFÔÇØ to disable the button power off functions.
+
+     Ô×ó Button power off ----- You will not be able to turn off the device through mechanical button once disabled.
+
+Figure 19: Disable ÔÇ£button power offÔÇØ function
+
+6.8 Remote turn off your PIR device
+
+In some cases that mechanical button function should be disabled to avoid intentional shut down situations, then we
+will need to remote shut down function for backup.
+Under this circumstance, you can remote turn off your Beacon by just tapping ÔÇ£DEVICE >>> Remote Power OFFÔÇØ
+option, then it will give prompt to you to confirm the shut down operations.
+
+Figure 20: Remote power off your PIR device
+
+           Notice ÔÇô Please make sure that device have mechanical button, or other ways to turn on before you do
+           remote shut down operations.
+
+6.9 Remote reset your PIR device
+
+In some cases that mechanical button function should be disabled or without mechanical button, to avoid intentional
+reset situations, then we will need remote reset function for backup. You can remote reset your Beacon by just
+tapping ÔÇ£DEVICE >>> Remote resetÔÇØ option, then it will give prompt to you to confirm the reset operations.
+Remark: Remote reset operations will not change the password to factory reset status. If you want to totally reset the
+Beacon with password restore as well, then you may need to do hardware reset operations.
+
+6.10 Check your device information
+
+In some cases, you will need to know the firmware version of Beacon, or product model and etc. You can access to
+ÔÇ£DeviceÔÇØ interface to check and confirm those essential information, which including battery voltage, running time
+since power on, MAC address and etc.
+

+6.11 Upgrade your deviceÔÇÖs firmware
+
+The Beacon can realize OTA firmware update through DFU operations. In the next we will describe the detailed steps
+based on android ÔÇ£MK PIRÔÇØ APP.
+Step 1: Copy the upgrade package to a folder in the root directory of the smartphone.
+Step 2: Connect with device and go to ÔÇ£SETTINGÔÇØ interface, press ÔÇ£OTA DFUÔÇØ option and then file manager route of
+
+         smartphone will be prompted.
+Step 3: Choose the required upgrade firmware package and the upgrade process will start automatically. Wait for
+
+         the upgrade to complete, APP will disconnect with Beacon after upgrade completed successfully.
+
+Figure 21: Firmware upgrade workflow of ÔÇ£MK PIRÔÇØ APP (Android)
+

-DOCUMENTACION DE COMANDOS ADC
-
-1. COMANDO SFIL (Set Filter)
-   - Descripci├│n: Configura los par├ímetros del filtro para un canal ADC espec├¡fico. Establece un valor m├¡nimo (m) y un valor de reemplazo (p) si la lectura es menor o igual a ese m├¡nimo.
-   - Sintaxis: DVL+SFIL=n,m,p
-   - Par├ímetros:
-     * n: ├ìndice del canal ADC (0, 1 o 2).
-     * m: Valor umbral m├¡nimo (float). Si la lectura es <= m, se aplica el filtro.
-     * p: Valor de reemplazo (float). Valor que tomar├í la lectura si es <= m.
-   - Ejemplo: DVL+SFIL=0,0.5,0.0
-     (Configura el canal 0: si la lectura es <= 0.5, el valor ser├í 0.0)
-   - Respuesta Exitosa: FILTRO SETEADO OK
-   - Errores Posibles:
-     * ERROR: Indice fuera de rango (0-2)
-     * ERROR: Formato incorrecto (n,m,p)
-
-2. COMANDO SFACTOR (Set Factor)
-   - Descripci├│n: Configura los par├ímetros de calibraci├│n lineal (pendiente y offset) para un canal ADC espec├¡fico. La f├│rmula aplicada es: ValorFinal = (ValorLeido * m) + p.
-   - Sintaxis: DVL+SFACTOR=n,m,p
-   - Par├ímetros:
-     * n: ├ìndice del canal ADC (0, 1 o 2).
-     * m: Factor multiplicativo o pendiente (float).
-     * p: Offset o desplazamiento (float).
-   - Ejemplo: DVL+SFACTOR=1,1.05,0.2
-     (Configura el canal 1: multiplica la lectura por 1.05 y suma 0.2)
-   - Respuesta Exitosa: FACTOR SETEADO OK
-   - Errores Posibles:
-     * ERROR: Indice fuera de rango (0-2)
-     * ERROR: Formato incorrecto (n,m,p)
-
-3. COMANDO SALI (Set Alisado)
-   - Descripci├│n: Configura la cantidad de muestras utilizadas para el promedio m├│vil (alisado) de las lecturas del ADC.
-   - Sintaxis: DVL+SALI=val
-   - Par├ímetros:
-     * val: N├║mero de muestras para el promedio (entero > 0).
-   - Ejemplo: DVL+SALI=50
-     (Establece que se promediar├ín las ├║ltimas 50 mediciones)
-   - Respuesta Exitosa: ALISADO SETEADO OK
-   - Errores Posibles:
-     * ERROR: Valor debe ser mayor a 0
-
-4. COMANDO STADC (Set Time ADC)
-   - Descripci├│n: Configura el intervalo de tiempo para la toma de muestras del ADC. El valor se multiplica por 100ms.
-   - Sintaxis: DVL+STADC=val
-   - Par├ímetros:
-     * val: Valor entero entre 1 y 65000. (Ej: 1 = 100ms, 10 = 1s, 600 = 1min).
-   - Ejemplo: DVL+STADC=10
-     (Establece que se tomar├í una muestra cada 1 segundo)
-   - Respuesta Exitosa: TIME ADC SETEADO OK
-   - Errores Posibles:
-     * ERROR: Valor fuera de rango (1-65000)
-
-NOTAS ADICIONALES:
-- Los pines de ADC son los siguientes: ADC0->35, ADC1->36, ADC2->39
-- Todos los cambios realizados con estos comandos se guardan en la memoria no vol├ítil (Preferences) y persisten tras reiniciar el dispositivo.
-- Para consultar los valores actuales se pueden usar los comandos:
-  * DVL+QFIL (Consulta filtros)
-  * DVL+QFACTOR (Consulta factores)
-  * DVL+QALI (Consulta alisado)
-  * DVL+QTADC (Consulta tiempo de muestreo)
